# 28 Forward X Failure First-6-Step Stage Detail Analysis

⚠️ STANDALONE MODE — `.oma/best.json` 缺失，本次按已有 deploy/forward_x_failure 上下文直接进入细粒度分析。

## Scope

- Data rerun scope: real 4 cases + sim 4 cases.
- Event scope: each case first 6 touchdown events, no all-step averaging used as the primary readout.
- Stage split: `swing = touchdown-350ms .. touchdown-20ms`, `touchdown = touchdown-50ms .. touchdown+100ms`.
- Delay analysis: only `pos_target(pos_des_raw) -> joint_pos(pos)` network lag.
- Jitter analysis: first align by the estimated delay, then read joint adjustment frequency, amplitude, and target-to-joint response.
- Touchdown posture: use `joint_pos` only; no `sole_pos`-based touchdown attitude metric is used in this report.

## Output Files

- `real2sim/table/forward_x_failure_first6/forward_x_failure_first6_delay_detail.csv`
- `real2sim/table/forward_x_failure_first6/forward_x_failure_first6_cycle_detail.csv`
- `real2sim/table/forward_x_failure_first6/forward_x_failure_first6_touchdown_posture.csv`
- `real2sim/table/forward_x_failure_first6/forward_x_failure_first6_phase_profiles.csv`
- `real2sim/table/forward_x_failure_first6/forward_x_failure_first6_cycle_histograms.csv`
